### E13-H72 capped-ground-cost

- attachment: ../../notebooks/experiments/E13-H72-kj-capped-ground-cost.ipynb sha256:0e0598e5a5077211 edited:2026-10-09T09:27:14Z
- attachment: ../../reports/E13-H72-capped-ground-cost-metrics.json sha256:25ed30b7cdcef517 edited:2026-10-09T09:27:11Z
- **Hypothesis** - because a statement with no counterpart pays the ground cost to whichever unrelated statement is nearest, the same edit costs more or less depending on unrelated content, so capping the ground cost at `c` (`min(sqrt(2 - 2cos), c)`, still a metric ground cost under the exact solve) will halve the spread of the cost of one inserted unrelated statement while the ibm panel stays unchanged
- **Lever** - ground-cost cap `c` on the semantic solve only; marginals, solver and the order axis held fixed
- **Mechanism** - with the cap every arc longer than `c` costs `c`, which equals discarding that mass at a fixed price (robust transport); below `c` nothing changes
- **Prediction** - ibm panel: max |delta smd| about 0.003, V 0/24; cross-article insertions: coefficient of variation of the per-insertion increment falls by more than 50%
- **Acceptance bar** - PASS iff V = 0/24 AND boundary margin >= +4.61 closeness pts AND triangle violations 0/495 AND the coefficient of variation of the smd increment per inserted cross-article statement falls >= 50% against shipped
- **Experiment** - fixture: ibm 11-summary panel (data/interim/exec-summaries/ibm-ai-adoption/summaries/*.md, anchor exec-summary-gold-opus-4-6.md) for the panel row; insertion set - each ibm gold-tier base doc receives one statement at a time from data/interim/ai-society-wergeland/source/source-article.md (every statement, one fixed position), and the reverse for the wergeland bases<br>method: ot.emd2 on np.minimum(cost, c); registered arm c = 0.6, c in {0.45, 0.75} reported without a bar<br>baseline: shipped smd<br>sanity: naive row on OpenVINO INT8 - margin +4.71, d' 4.68, V 0/24 (the E12-H66 reading)<br>artefacts: notebooks/experiments/E13-H72-kj-capped-ground-cost.ipynb, reports/E13-H72-capped-ground-cost-metrics.json, logs/E13-H72-capped-ground-cost.log<br>model: opus
- **Kill-gate** - share of cross-article inserted statements whose cheapest cost to the host document exceeds 0.6 - below 50% the cap rarely binds and the lever is dead
- **Evidence** - src/docdistance/distance.py:43-58; sizing probe 2026-10-07 on the 55 ibm plans: no arc above 0.592, max |delta| 0.0025 under a 0.6 cap, margin +4.708 reproduced
- **Result** - Sanity check passed (V 0/24, margin +4.708, d' 4.684). Kill-gate fired: 172/574 = 30.0% of cross-article insertions have a cheapest cost to the host document above 0.6 (19.2% with an ibm host, 48.6% with a wergeland host, 5.2% on statements of 5+ words); the record requires at least 50%. Readings after the gate, which do not decide the verdict: at c = 0.60 the ibm panel keeps V 0/24 and margin +4.708 with max |delta smd| 0.0025 and 0/495 triangle violations; the pooled CV of the per-insertion increment falls 0.3957 → 0.1189 (70.0%), and that fall is produced by inserted statements under 5 words (mean cost to the host 1.097, capped to 0.603); on statements of 5+ words the CV falls 15.6%. The increment SMD(B, B + s) equals the mean cost from the inserted statement to all n host statements divided by n + 1 (closed form checked on 574 insertions), so the cap changes 511/574 increments while the gate, which takes the cheapest arc, counts 172
- **Verdict** - Killed-at-gate; 30.0% of cross-article insertions cost above 0.6 to their nearest host statement, the gate requires at least 50%
- **Log**

### E13-H73 nearest-source-residual

- attachment: ../../notebooks/experiments/E13-H73-kj-nearest-source-residual.ipynb sha256:199ef6e4630cf3d9 edited:2026-10-09T09:48:22Z
- attachment: ../../reports/E13-H73-nearest-source-residual-metrics.json sha256:e2254d7efd7181be edited:2026-10-09T09:48:19Z
- **Hypothesis** - because `ungrounded_residual` runs balanced transport against the source, a statement keeps at most n/m of its mass on its nearest source statement (DEF-TRANS-8: 10 verbatim source statements score closeness 0.322), the one-sided residual - mean cost to the nearest source statement - will read a verbatim extract at closeness >= 0.99, rise with inserted foreign statements and stay flat under deletions
- **Lever** - source-side marginal released on the document-to-source residual only; `D_sel`, the grounding cascade and the symmetric distance held fixed
- **Mechanism** - with only the document marginal enforced each statement ships to its nearest source statement, so the residual measures unsupported content and omission stays on the selection axis
- **Prediction** - verbatim extract 0.322 -> >= 0.99; Spearman(insertion dose, residual) >= 0.95; deletions move it by under 10% of one insertion
- **Acceptance bar** - PASS iff verbatim-extract closeness >= 0.99 AND median per-document Spearman(k foreign insertions, residual) >= 0.95 over k in 0..4 AND median |delta residual| under k in 1..4 deletions <= 10% of the mean one-insertion effect; the balanced residual is scored on the same doses as the baseline
- **Experiment** - fixture: ibm source (70 statements) and the 11 summaries; foreign statements from the wergeland source; doses built in the notebook<br>method: residual = cost_matrix(X, S).min(1).mean(), with and without the shipped anisotropy pre-pass (on by default in compute_source_conditioned)<br>baseline: shipped ungrounded_residual on the same doses<br>also reported, no bar: tier readings and Spearman against the model-graded residual recomputed from data/interim/E12-H69-grounding-signals.npz<br>artefacts: notebooks/experiments/E13-H73-kj-nearest-source-residual.ipynb, reports/E13-H73-nearest-source-residual-metrics.json, logs/E13-H73-nearest-source-residual.log<br>model: opus
- **Kill-gate** - statement-level AUC of nearest-source cost, the summaries' own statements against foreign (wergeland) statements, measured against the ibm source - below 0.90 the geometry carries no grounding signal
- **Evidence** - src/docdistance/distance.py:304-311 and 470-525; docs/defects-docdistance.md DEF-TRANS-8; sizing probe 2026-10-07: 44-51% of mass and 70-74% of cost on arcs above 0.6 across the 11 summary-vs-source plans
- **Result** - Sanity check: DEF-TRANS-8 reproduced (residual_a 0.958, closeness_a 0.322; each copied statement keeps 0.142857 = n/m of its mass on its own copy). Kill-gate (statement-level AUC of nearest-source cost, 98 own against 38 wergeland statements, gate 0.90): 0.9670 with the anisotropy pre-pass off, 0.7889 with it on; the record names no pre-pass setting, the run was read on the passing setting (pre-pass off), and on the default call alone it is Killed-at-gate. Condition (i), met by construction: verbatim-extract closeness 0.9999 one-sided against 0.3224 (balanced, pre-pass on) and 0.6343 (balanced, pre-pass off). Condition (ii) met: median per-document Spearman of the residual against k foreign insertions 1.0000 with the pre-pass off (0.9492 under a pooled-points reading) and 0.9643 with it on; the shipped balanced residual reads -0.2571 and -0.8429 on the same doses, so it falls when foreign statements are added. Condition (iii) not met: the median |residual change| under 1 to 4 deletions is 34.1% of one insertion with the pre-pass off (310.4% with it on), bar 10%; the mean signed change is -0.7% of one insertion; the per-statement cost spread is 0.0532 against a gap to the foreign costs of 0.1197. No bar: tier means gold 0.2829, adv1 0.3561, adv2 0.3185; Spearman against the model-graded residual +0.4182 over the 11 documents. Guardrails: V 0/24, default-path and source-conditioned outputs bit-identical before and after; the one-sided residual is a lower bound of the balanced one (0 violations in 1166 scored documents)
- **Verdict** - Refuted; deletion effect 34.1% of one insertion against a bar of 10%, read with the pre-pass off - conditions (i) 0.9999 and (ii) 1.0000 are met

### E13-H74 order-kernel-reach

- attachment: ../../notebooks/experiments/E13-H74-kj-order-kernel-reach.ipynb sha256:bb4a8c50411cfb49 edited:2026-10-09T09:21:03Z
- attachment: ../../reports/E13-H74-order-kernel-reach-metrics.json sha256:7a5c0151b70ae224 edited:2026-10-09T09:21:00Z
- **Hypothesis** - because the OPW log-kernel falls as -(i-j)^2/4 at equal lengths under the shipped sigma 1, its float64 value is exactly zero beyond about 54 positions, so on long documents the order gap will stop growing once a block moves further than that while it still grows inside the band
- **Lever** - none - a measurement of shipped `opw_gap` on long sequences
- **Mechanism** - the coupling cannot carry mass to a counterpart outside the kernel support, so a far move costs the same as a move to the band edge
- **Prediction** - growth >= 20% from 5 to 50 positions, within 5% from 60 to 200
- **Acceptance bar** - Confirmed iff, on sequences of >= 300 statements, the median order gap grows >= 20% between displacement 5 and 50 AND changes <= 5% between 60 and 200 (one block of 10 statements moved); Refuted if it still grows >= 20% between 60 and 200
- **Experiment** - fixture: sequences of 300 (and 600 if the pool allows) unique statements pooled from data/interim/01-statements.parquet (163), the two source articles and the fixture summaries, embedded once with the shipped encoder; 5 seeds per length<br>method: move one block of 10 statements by d in {5, 10, 20, 50, 60, 100, 200} positions, shipped opw_gap(A, A_moved); repeated with statements kept in their original document order to rule out a shuffled-pool artefact<br>artefacts: notebooks/experiments/E13-H74-kj-order-kernel-reach.ipynb, reports/E13-H74-order-kernel-reach-metrics.json, logs/E13-H74-order-kernel-reach.log<br>model: opus
- **Kill-gate** - read the kernel `K` inside `_opw_plan` for N = M = 300 - if entries at |i-j| = 60 are non-zero the premise is false
- **Evidence** - src/docdistance/distance.py:145-173; the ibm and wergeland fixtures hold at most 14 statements per document, so the order axis has no long-document reading
- **Result** - Kill-gate passed: every kernel entry at |i - j| = 60 is exactly 0.0 for N = M = 300, the widest non-zero offset is 54. Median shipped opw_gap on 300-statement sequences with one block of 10 statements moved (5 seeds): shuffled arm 0.03228 at displacement 5, 0.12482 at 50, 0.14324 at 60, 0.41615 at 200 (+286.6% from 5 to 50, +190.5% from 60 to 200); document-order control +315.1% and +188.7%; 10/10 sequences grow >= 20% from 60 to 200. Row-group split: the gap is linear in the d + 10 displaced statements (slope 0.5833 per displaced fraction, R^2 0.999); the 10 block rows cost 0.02023 at d = 10 and 0.02027 at d = 200, the growth comes from the passed-over rows (0.11992 → 0.39295). opw_gap(A, A) reads a median 0.00655 (shuffled) and 0.00995 (document order). The 600-statement length was not run: the pool holds 372 unique statements. Guardrails: V 0/24, default path equal, src, tests and docs trees unchanged
- **Verdict** - Refuted; the median order gap grows +190.5% from displacement 60 to 200 (0.14324 → 0.41615), the bar allows a change of at most 5%
- **Log**

  - log: 2026-10-09 @kj - verdict recorded: Refuted
  - log: 2026-10-09 @kj - verifier accepted with minors - one moved block shifts the statements it passes over, so the registered design varies the count of displaced statements together with the distance and the label covers the registered prediction only; the executor named the shuffled arm as the primary one

Three limits were measured outside the bars and decide what a build needs. Repeated statements make the one-to-one matching non-unique: on the 62-statement source document with 9 repeated statements the survivors of a pure deletion are recovered in 88/120 cases (E13-H70), which E15-H79 addresses with a tie-break. The identity floor `opw_gap(P, P)` of the shipped order gap is not zero and grows with length (5.182e-03 at 30 statements, 6.786e-03 at 53), so the matched order gap of a pure deletion equals that floor, not zero. The nearest-source cost separates own from foreign statements on raw embeddings only (E13-H73), which E15-H78 carries forward as a count.

## E14 - anisotropy-centering-transfer

Three hypotheses, the first two opened because acceptance criterion `ACC-GEOM-21` (the conditioned-path anisotropy pre-pass becomes centering plus renormalisation) ships only after a second fixture reproduces the E12-H64 attribution. E12-H64 measured the attribution on the ibm fixture alone, with one anchor. E14-H75 repeats the three-arm decomposition on the Wergeland article sections and their back-translation paraphrases, a pool that shares no document with the ibm set. Measurement only: no edit under `src/`, default-path `smd` / `order_gap` bit-identical. Pre-registered 2026-10-09 in the E13 environment (Python 3.14.8, POT 0.9.6.post1, OpenVINO 2026.2.1). Unrun at registration - Result and Verdict follow execution.

E14-H76 was registered the same day, after E14-H75 returned: its reading without a bar showed that centering alone changes the conditioned-path `D_sel` to 0.33 to 0.49 times the shipped value on three triples. E14-H76 measures whether centering alone keeps the E04-H15 property (`D_sel` dynamic range at 0 selection-axis violations) on the path `ACC-GEOM-21` would change, and its bar carries the decision rule for that criterion.

E14-H77 was registered the same day on the Star Colonel's question whether a kernel-based PCA would serve as the pre-pass. It is a probe with a kill-gate: if the kernel map only reproduces the linear pre-pass, no panel is scored.

### E14-H75 centering-attribution-transfer

- attachment: ../../notebooks/experiments/E14-H75-kj-centering-attribution-transfer.ipynb sha256:77c2a40fa1c6bbda edited:2026-10-09T09:24:00Z
- attachment: ../../reports/E14-H75-centering-attribution-transfer-metrics.json sha256:69ce07cb10ab4e3a edited:2026-10-09T09:23:57Z
- **Hypothesis** - because E12-H64 measured on the ibm fixture that the uncentered top singular vector of the pooled statement matrix is the mean direction (cosine 0.999999) and that centering plus re-L2-normalisation alone carries 103.7% of the margin gain of the shipped all_but_the_top(k=1), the same decomposition on a fixture that shares no document with the ibm set (the three Wergeland article sections and their back-translation paraphrases) will again show centering plus renormalisation recovering at least 60% of the full-composition margin gain while the clean projection isolate recovers less than 30% - the attribution is a property of mmBERT sentence-embedding geometry, not of one article
- **Lever** - decomposition of the shipped all_but_the_top(k=1) into the three E12-H64 arms - (a) center + renorm, (b) center, remove PC1, restore the mean, renorm, (c) the full shipped composition - with the fixture changed and everything else held fixed; measurement only
- **Mechanism** - mean-pooled, L2-normalised mmBERT statement embeddings share one large common mean vector; subtracting it removes the cosine compression, and the top principal component of the centered matrix then carries topic content of the pooled documents, so projecting it out adds nothing to the separation of same-content from different-content pairs
- **Prediction** - pooled mean norm above 0.9; cos(uncentered PC1, mean direction) at least 0.999; arm a recovers at least 90% of the full-composition margin gain; arm b recovers between -15% and +15%; V 0/12 in arms a and c
- **Acceptance bar** - attribution reproduces iff exactly one arm reaches >= 60% of the full-composition margin gain at V = 0/12, that arm is (a), and arm b stays below 30% - the E12-H64 bar applied to the second fixture. Guardrails: raw embeddings byte-identical after every arm, default-path smd and order_gap bit-identical (no edit under src/), unit norms and cost = sqrt(2 - 2cos) to 1e-6 in every arm
- **Experiment** - fixture: data/processed/structure-fixture/statements.json - werg-a, werg-b, werg-c (11, 13, 14 statements) and werg-a~bt, werg-b~bt, werg-c~bt, 76 pooled statements, no ibm document in the estimation pool or the scored pairs; encoder: shipped OpenVINO INT8 mmBERT<br>panel: for each of the three anchors the positive is its own paraphrase and the negatives are the other two sections and their paraphrases - 3 positives, 12 negatives; V = number of (positive, negative) orderings violated within an anchor (0/12 is clean); margin = min positive closeness minus max negative closeness over the pooled panel, in closeness points; d' between the two populations<br>arms: naive (no pre-pass), a, b, c, estimation pool = the 76 pooled statements; recovery of an arm = (its margin - naive margin) / (arm c margin - naive margin)<br>reported without a bar: cos(uncentered PC1, mean direction), pooled mean norm, the ibm panel under arm a (the E12-H64 reading +19.95 pts, V 0/24) as the harness sanity check, and the largest absolute difference in conditioned-path closeness between arm a and arm c on the three ibm grounded triples of the E13 baseline<br>sanity: E12-H64 ibm rows on OpenVINO INT8 within 5% - naive margin +4.71, arm a +19.95, full composition +19.41<br>artefacts: notebooks/experiments/E14-H75-kj-centering-attribution-transfer.ipynb, reports/E14-H75-centering-attribution-transfer-metrics.json, logs/E14-H75-centering-attribution-transfer.log<br>model: opus
- **Kill-gate** - two readings on the re-encoded second fixture before the panel run: pooled mean norm below 0.15 (centering cannot be the cause), or a full-composition margin gain below +1.0 closeness point over naive (no gain to attribute on this fixture) - either one kills the hypothesis
- **Evidence** - docs/experiments/wmd-docdistance-experiments.md E12-H64 record (arm a 103.7%, arm b -4.5%, cos 0.999999, one fixture, one anchor); E12-H65 (minimum estimation pool about 71 statements); docs/acc-crit-docdistance.md ACC-GEOM-21 (ships only after a second fixture reproduces the attribution); src/docdistance/distance.py:94-115 (all_but_the_top)
- **Result** - Kill-gate passed: pooled mean norm 0.9476 on the 76 Wergeland statements, full-composition margin gain +38.90 closeness points over naive. Sanity check: the E12-H64 ibm rows on OpenVINO INT8 within 0.04% (naive +4.7083, arm a +19.9526, full composition +19.4144, V 0/24). Second fixture (3 positives, 12 negatives): margin naive +15.84, arm a (center + renorm) +55.83, arm b (projection isolate) +14.45, full composition +54.74, V 0/12 in all four; arm a recovers 102.8% of the full-composition gain and arm b -3.6% (ibm: 103.7% and -4.5%); cos(uncentered PC1, mean direction) 0.999999, cos(centered PC1, mean direction) 0.056; 0/15 pairs clipped. Conditioned-path reading without a bar: with the pre-pass replaced by arm a on three ibm grounded triples D_sel is 0.330 to 0.493 times the shipped value with the order of the triples preserved, largest closeness difference 7.26 points. Guardrails: raw embeddings byte-identical after each arm, default-path smd and order_gap bit-identical on 15 + 55 pairs, unit norms to 2.4e-07
- **Verdict** - Confirmed; arm a recovers 102.8% of the full-composition margin gain on the Wergeland pool at V 0/12, arm b -3.6%

### E14-H76 conditioned-centering-probe

- attachment: ../../notebooks/experiments/E14-H76-kj-conditioned-centering-probe.ipynb sha256:270aa83fb19a3b9c edited:2026-10-09T10:29:14Z
- attachment: ../../reports/E14-H76-conditioned-centering-probe-metrics.json sha256:2f4e5f579bcec8a8 edited:2026-10-09T10:29:11Z
- **Hypothesis** - because the conditioned path estimates the pre-pass from one triple (document, gold, source - about 90 statements, three quarters of them source statements) and the first principal component of that centered pool is nearly orthogonal to the mean direction (E14-H75: cosine 0.056), the projection step removes content variance of the source there, so centering plus renormalisation is not interchangeable with the shipped all_but_the_top(k=1) on the conditioned selection axis: it will lose more than 10% of the D_sel dynamic range the shipped pre-pass delivers (E04-H15), even though the two are interchangeable on the symmetric panels (E12-H64, E14-H75)
- **Lever** - probe, no lever - the pre-pass of compute_source_conditioned swapped between none, the shipped all_but_the_top(k=1) and centering plus renormalisation (arm a), everything downstream held fixed; measurement only
- **Mechanism** - on a symmetric multi-document panel the centered first component is dominated by between-document content the panel does not need, so dropping the projection costs nothing; on a single triple the pool is dominated by one source and the coverage profile is built against that same source, so projecting out the source's first content component changes which source statements each summary statement is nearest to
- **Prediction** - all three arms read 0 selection-axis violations; DR(D_sel) shipped at least 1.5x the raw arm (the E04-H15 property on the shipped INT8 path); DR(D_sel) under arm a between 0.3x and 0.9x of the shipped arm
- **Acceptance bar** - Confirmed (not interchangeable) iff DR(D_sel) under arm a is below 0.9x the shipped arm OR arm a reads at least one selection-axis violation; Refuted (interchangeable) iff arm a reads 0 violations AND DR(D_sel) under arm a is at least 0.9x the shipped arm. Decision rule recorded with the bar: ACC-GEOM-21 changes the conditioned-path pre-pass only on Refuted; on Confirmed the shipped pre-pass stays. Guardrails: default-path smd and order_gap bit-identical (no edit under src/), raw embeddings byte-identical after every arm, D_grd untouched
- **Experiment** - fixture: the 11 ibm summaries against the anchor exec-summary-gold-opus-4-6.md with source data/interim/exec-summaries/ibm-ai-adoption/source/source-article.md, shipped OpenVINO INT8 mmBERT, the shipped per-triple pool {document, anchor, source}<br>panel: D_sel per non-anchor document from shipped compute_source_conditioned; DR(D_sel) and the selection-axis violation count defined exactly as in notebooks/experiments/E04-kj-source-conditioned-performance.ipynb (E04-H15), quoted in the notebook before any number is computed<br>arms: raw (anisotropy=False), shipped (anisotropy=True), arm a (center + renorm applied to the pooled triple outside the function, then anisotropy=False)<br>reported without a bar: Spearman of D_sel between arm a and shipped over the 10 documents, the gold-to-adversarial contrast per arm, closeness_a and residual_a per arm, the same three arms on the Wergeland source with the three Wergeland sections<br>artefacts: notebooks/experiments/E14-H76-kj-conditioned-centering-probe.ipynb, reports/E14-H76-conditioned-centering-probe-metrics.json, logs/E14-H76-conditioned-centering-probe.log<br>model: opus
- **Kill-gate** - harness check on the shipped arm before the comparison: DR(D_sel) shipped must be at least 1.5x the raw arm at 0 violations on the shipped INT8 path with the per-triple pool (the E04-H15 acceptance bar); if the shipped pre-pass does not show that property here, the panel is not the one E04-H15 scored and the probe is killed
- **Evidence** - E14-H75 conditioned-path reading without a bar: arm a against the shipped pre-pass on three ibm triples - D_sel at 0.330 to 0.493 times the shipped value, largest closeness difference 7.26 points, order of the three triples preserved; cos(centered PC1, mean direction) 0.056; docs/experiments/wmd-docdistance-experiments.md E04-H15 record (DR 0.0370 -> 0.1253 at 0 violations, pooled {A, B, S}); src/docdistance/distance.py compute_source_conditioned; docs/acc-crit-docdistance.md ACC-GEOM-21
- **Result** - Kill-gate passed on the shipped INT8 path, pool {document, anchor, source}, 10 ibm triples: DR(D_sel) raw 0.034535, shipped 0.219067 = 6.3433x raw (gate 1.5x) at 0/28 selection-axis violations. Arm a (center + renorm) reads DR(D_sel) 0.119861 = 0.5471x shipped (bar: below 0.9x) and 1/28 violations (haiku 0.140584 >= adv2-b 0.136424); per document D_sel under arm a is 0.330 to 0.502 times the shipped value, Spearman with shipped 0.9758. Prediction clauses: shipped / raw at least 1.5x held; arm a / shipped inside 0.3x to 0.9x held; 0 violations in every arm falsified - predicted 0, observed 1 in arm a. Three of the 10 triples had been read at E14-H75 before registration; on the 7 documents not read before, arm a / shipped is 0.5390 and arm a reads 0/10 gold-adversarial violations. The registered mechanism is not what the outputs show: the default reader segments the ibm source into 70 statements of which 22 are one word ('-' 13, '###' 6, '##' 2, 'Overview' 1); the centered PC1 carries 0.6835 to 0.6921 of the variance and separates those 22 (coordinate +0.709 to +0.868) from every document statement (-0.443 to -0.230); cos(centered PC1, mean direction) is 0.2499 to 0.3091, registered 0.056. Readings without a bar, chosen after the first execution: arm a / shipped 0.9165 at 0/28 with the 22 one-word source statements removed, 1.7248 at 0/28 with the 28 statements under 5 words removed, 0.9359 at 0/28 on the E04 pool of 168 statements; on the three Wergeland triples D_sel under arm a is 0.930 to 0.944 times shipped. Scale-free contrast: arm a 2.8565, shipped 2.4134. Guardrails: V 0/24, raw embeddings byte-identical after each arm, default-path smd and order_gap bit-identical on 55 pairs, D_grd bit-identical across arms, src and tests unchanged
- **Verdict** - Confirmed; not interchangeable on the registered fixture - arm a reads 0.5471x the shipped DR(D_sel) (bar: below 0.9x; 0.5390 on the 7 documents not read before registration) and 1/28 violations. ### E14-H77 kernel-pca-prepass

- attachment: ../../notebooks/experiments/E14-H77-kj-kernel-pca-prepass.ipynb sha256:00e344cd3e65fe77 edited:2026-10-09T11:08:58Z
- attachment: ../../reports/E14-H77-kernel-pca-prepass-metrics.json sha256:f3bcfba190f30348 edited:2026-10-09T11:04:18Z
- **Hypothesis** - because every kernel on unit vectors is a function of the cosine, and mmBERT statement cosines occupy a narrow band (pooled mean off-diagonal cosine 0.736 on the ibm triples), a smooth kernel is close to an affine function of the cosine over that band, so kernel PCA with an RBF kernel at the median-heuristic bandwidth returns the same common-mode components as the shipped linear all_but_the_top(k=1) and cannot lift the conditioned selection axis or the symmetric panel; a kernel narrow enough to differ estimates its components from about 90 statements and loses stability instead
- **Lever** - probe - the pre-pass replaced by the empirical kernel map: Gram matrix of the pooled statements under k(x, y) = exp(-gamma * (2 - 2cos)), double-centered, eigendecomposed, coordinates = eigenvectors scaled by the square root of their eigenvalues, the top-1 coordinate dropped, rows re-L2-normalised; the resulting explicit vectors feed the shipped functions unchanged. The bandwidth is never optimised: gamma takes five pre-determined values, m / (median squared chordal distance of the pool) for m in {0.25, 1, 4, 16, 64}, the same grid on every pool; measurement only
- **Mechanism** - double-centering removes the constant term of the kernel, so wherever the kernel is affine in the cosine the centered Gram matrix is a scalar multiple of the centered linear Gram matrix and has the same eigenvectors; the cost on the mapped vectors stays sqrt(2 - 2cos) of unit vectors in a Hilbert space, so the metric property of the ground cost is kept
- **Prediction** - for m = 0.25 and m = 1 the pairwise statement costs under the kernel map and under the shipped pre-pass correlate at Spearman >= 0.995 on every pool (monotone copies, not scored); for m = 16 and m = 64 the map departs from the linear one and the bootstrap stability of its top component falls below 0.9; no grid bandwidth meets the bar
- **Acceptance bar** - Confirmed (kernel PCA lifts the pre-pass) iff at least one grid bandwidth that passes the gate reads, with that one bandwidth: 0 selection-axis violations and a gold-to-adversarial contrast at least 10% above the shipped pre-pass on the ibm conditioned panel, AND V 0/24 with margin not more than 1 closeness point below the shipped pre-pass margin (+19.41) on the ibm symmetric panel, AND V 0/12 with margin not more than 1 closeness point below the full composition (+54.74) on the Wergeland panel - the second article guards against the best of five bandwidths being chance on one fixture. Refuted iff at least one bandwidth passes the gate and none meets every clause. Guardrails: the kernel map with the linear kernel reproduces the shipped all_but_the_top(k=1) pairwise costs to 1e-5 (harness check), default-path smd and order_gap bit-identical (no edit under src/), raw embeddings byte-identical after every arm
- **Experiment** - fixtures: the ibm conditioned panel of E14-H76 (10 documents against the anchor, per-triple pool, D_sel, dynamic range, violations and contrast as E04-H15 defines them), the ibm symmetric panel (98 pooled statements, margin, d', V), and the 76-statement Wergeland panel of E14-H75<br>arms: raw, shipped linear pre-pass, kernel map with the linear kernel (harness check), and for each of the five grid bandwidths the RBF kernel map with the top-1 coordinate dropped and the same map without dropping (the kernel analogue of centering alone)<br>per bandwidth, reported whether or not it is scored: cost Spearman against the shipped pre-pass, top-eigenvalue share of the centered Gram matrix, bootstrap stability of the top kernel component over 50 statement resamples with a fixed seed<br>every bandwidth that passes the gate is scored on all three panels; the full table of five is reported, never only the best row<br>artefacts: notebooks/experiments/E14-H77-kj-kernel-pca-prepass.ipynb, reports/E14-H77-kernel-pca-prepass-metrics.json, logs/E14-H77-kernel-pca-prepass.log<br>model: opus
- **Kill-gate** - per bandwidth: Spearman between the pairwise statement costs under the RBF kernel map (top-1 dropped) and under the shipped linear pre-pass, read on the ibm symmetric pool and on each of the 10 ibm conditioned triples - at or above 0.995 on all of them that bandwidth is a monotone copy of the linear pre-pass and is not scored. The probe is killed only if all five grid bandwidths are monotone copies
- **Evidence** - Star Colonel's question of 2026-10-09 (kernel-based PCA as the pre-pass); E04-H15 (pooled mean off-diagonal cosine 0.736, linear k swept 1 to 3); E12-H64 and E14-H75 (first uncentered singular value 8.26 to 9.44 against 0.85 to 1.51 for the second - the common mode is one linear direction); E12-H65 (the linear top-1 direction needs about 71 pooled statements); E09-H51 (bootstrap stability 0.77 to 0.86 on small pools)
- **Result** - Harness check passed: the kernel map with the linear kernel reproduces the statement costs of the shipped all_but_the_top(k=1) to 4.26e-07 at most on 13 pools (bar 1e-05; read in float64 on exact-unit copies - on the float32 arrays as fed the reading is 9.13e-04, and the shipped cost_matrix differs from itself in float64 by 8.46e-04). Kill-gate passed for all five bandwidths: lowest cost Spearman against the shipped pre-pass over the 11 gate pools 0.9816, 0.9091, 0.5087, 0.0712, -0.0664 for m = 0.25, 1, 4, 16, 64, none at or above 0.995, so none is a monotone copy and all five were scored. With the top-1 coordinate dropped: conditioned contrast 2.489, 2.494, 2.336, 1.913, 0.269 against shipped 2.413 and the required 2.655 (1.10x), selection-axis violations 0, 0, 0, 4, 22 of 28; ibm symmetric margin +19.04, +16.25, +6.11, +0.00, -9.54 against shipped +19.41 (V 0/24 for m = 0.25 to 16, 12/24 for m = 64); Wergeland margin +53.90, +50.26, +33.00, +6.78, -1.60 against shipped +54.74 (V 0/12 for m = 0.25 to 16, 2/12 for m = 64). 0 of 5 bandwidths meet every clause; m = 0.25 meets the two margin clauses and reads contrast 1.031x. Second readings give the same outcome: with nothing dropped m = 0.25 reads 1.116x with 2/28 violations; the registered margins change no clause. Prediction: no bandwidth meets the bar - held; m = 16 and m = 64 depart from the linear map - held; m = 0.25 and m = 1 are monotone copies - not held (0.9816 and 0.9091); stability below 0.9 for m = 16 and m = 64 - split: held on the ibm symmetric pool (0.183, 0.122) and the Wergeland pool (0.098, 0.099), not held on any of the 10 triples (0.955 to 0.977). Mechanism readings: at m = 0.25 the kernel is close to affine in the cosine (R2 0.9927) and its dropped coordinate agrees with the linear one at 0.9998; narrower kernels put most statement pairs at nearly the same distance (R2 0.3723, 0.0464, 0.0356 for m = 4, 16, 64). Without a bar, E14-H76 word rules: with the 28 source statements under 5 words removed the nothing-dropped arms read linear 1.206x, m = 0.25 1.193x, m = 1 1.109x at 0/28, so that gain is centering alone. Guardrails registered in the record and the primer hold: V 0/24 on the raw and shipped rows, raw embeddings byte-identical after 197 arm steps, default-path smd and order_gap bit-identical on 55 + 15 pairs, src and tests unchanged. The notebook's own triangle reading through cost_matrix (tolerance 1e-09, not registered) is exceeded on 11 of 12 kernel arms (+5.3e-09 to +3.7e-08); computed from coordinate differences the slack is +0.0e+00 on all 182 geometries
- **Verdict** - Refuted; 0 of 5 pre-determined RBF bandwidths meet every clause - the nearest, m = 0.25, reads conditioned contrast 1.031x the shipped pre-pass against the required 1.10x. The lever is refuted; the hypothesis forecast that no bandwidth meets the bar

Two readings carry over to the build. The attribution of the pre-pass gain differs by path: on symmetric panels the mean subtraction carries it on both articles, on the conditioned path of the ibm fixture the projection step is needed, and what it removes there is the direction of the one-word markup statements the plain reader, the default up to build 1.1.24, produces (DEF-SEGM-12). A non-linear kernel adds nothing at any pre-determined bandwidth: a wide kernel reproduces the linear map and a narrow one flattens the distances.

## E15 - overflow-fix-followups

Three follow-ups the E13 verdicts and the re-execution of E12-H61 call for before anything is built. E15-H78 re-states the source-residual lever that E13-H73 refuted on its deletion clause: the reading becomes a count on raw embeddings, with the cutoff fixed on calibration documents and a second article as transfer test - it decides the fix of DEF-TRANS-8. E15-H79 measures the two things the E13-H70 repair found missing for a build: a tie-break for repeated statements and one matching cutoff (`c = 0.325`, the E13-H71 value) shared by the per-statement map and the matched order gap. E15-H80, registered the same day after the re-executed E12-H61 read a different count of removed statements flagged `moved` (DEF-TRANS-13), tests the `moved` flag read on one-to-one matched pairs only. Standing guardrails on all three: `V` stays `0/24` on the ibm panel, default-path `smd` / `order_gap` bit-identical (no edit under `src/`), metric property untouched. Pre-registered 2026-10-09, same environment as E13. Unrun at registration - Result and Verdict follow execution.

### E15-H78 raw-nearest-source-count

- attachment: ../../notebooks/experiments/E15-H78-kj-raw-nearest-source-count.ipynb sha256:90f98dd523ff6798 edited:2026-10-09T11:09:21Z
- attachment: ../../reports/E15-H78-raw-nearest-source-count-metrics.json sha256:3660ffb48c2ec108 edited:2026-10-09T11:09:18Z
- **Hypothesis** - because the cost from a statement to its nearest source statement separates the statements of the source's own summaries from statements of another article at AUC 0.9670 on raw embeddings and at 0.7889 after the anisotropy pre-pass (E13-H73), a source residual read on raw embeddings as the count of statements whose nearest-source cost exceeds a cutoff fixed on calibration documents will flag inserted foreign statements on held-out documents at balanced accuracy >= 0.90, read 0 flagged statements on the verbatim extract of DEF-TRANS-8, and keep balanced accuracy >= 0.85 on a second source article with the cutoff unchanged; the count cannot move when supported statements are deleted, which is the clause E13-H73 failed with a mean
- **Lever** - source residual of the conditioned path - nearest-source cost per statement on raw embeddings (the anisotropy pre-pass is not applied to this reading), reported as a count above one cutoff and as a mean; the shipped balanced residual is the baseline
- **Mechanism** - one-sided: each statement is compared with its nearest source statement only, so no mass is forced onto unrelated source statements (the DEF-TRANS-8 root cause); a count over statements changes only when a statement that crosses the cutoff is added or removed
- **Prediction** - calibrated cutoff between 0.33 and 0.45; held-out balanced accuracy 0.90 to 0.96 in both directions; second-article balanced accuracy 0.85 to 0.95; the shipped balanced residual falls under the same insertions (E13-H73 read Spearman -0.2571)
- **Acceptance bar** - PASS iff, with the cutoff chosen on the calibration half of the 7 gold-tier ibm summaries (split by document, both directions scored): (1) held-out balanced accuracy of the flag, own gold-tier statements against inserted Wergeland statements of 5+ words, >= 0.90 in both directions; (2) the 10-statement verbatim extract source[0::7][:10] reads 0 flagged statements; (3) with the mean of the two cutoffs and no refit, on the Wergeland source: statements of werg-a, werg-b, werg-c against ibm gold-tier statements, balanced accuracy >= 0.85
- **Experiment** - fixture: ibm summaries and source (data/interim/exec-summaries/ibm-ai-adoption), gold tier = the 7 non-adversarial summaries, split 4 / 3 by document with the anchor in half A; foreign statements = Wergeland source statements of 5+ words (the E13-H73 foreign set, 38 statements); second article = data/interim/ai-society-wergeland/source/source-article.md with the keys werg-a, werg-b, werg-c of data/processed/structure-fixture/statements.json; encoder: shipped OpenVINO INT8, raw embeddings<br>method: nearest-source cost per statement = min over source statements of sqrt(2 - 2cos); cutoff = the grid point (step 0.005) that maximises calibration balanced accuracy, midpoint of the longest run at the maximum<br>reported without a bar: the count against k = 1 to 4 inserted foreign statements per held-out document (share of trials where the count rises by exactly k), the count under 1 to 4 deletions (0 by construction, stated as such), the mean nearest-source cost per tier, the 4 adversarial summaries scored against the per-statement entailment of data/interim/E12-H69-grounding-signals.npz (AUC of nearest-source cost against 1 - entailment), every reading repeated with source statements under 5 words removed, and the same flag computed after the shipped pre-pass<br>baseline: shipped compute_source_conditioned residual_a<br>artefacts: notebooks/experiments/E15-H78-kj-raw-nearest-source-count.ipynb, reports/E15-H78-raw-nearest-source-count-metrics.json, logs/E15-H78-raw-nearest-source-count.log<br>model: opus
- **Kill-gate** - AUC of nearest-source cost, own gold-tier statements against the foreign set, on each calibration half on raw embeddings: below 0.90 on either half no cutoff can reach the bar and the hypothesis is killed
- **Evidence** - E13-H73 (AUC 0.9670 raw and 0.7889 after the pre-pass; verbatim extract 0.9999 one-sided against 0.3224 shipped; shipped residual falls under foreign insertions; a mean moves 34.1% of one insertion under deletions); E14-H76 (22 of the 70 ibm source statements are one-word markup under the shipped reader); docs/defects-docdistance.md DEF-TRANS-8
- **Result** - Kill-gate passed: AUC of nearest-source cost, own gold-tier statements against the 38 foreign statements, raw embeddings, 0.9871 on half A (gold, v2, sonnet, haiku) and 0.9791 on half B (gold-2, v1, opus), gate 0.90. Harness: 7 of 7 numbers of the Evidence line reproduce. Clause (1) met: cutoffs 0.3375 (from half A) and 0.3550 (from half B), held-out balanced accuracy 0.9412 and 0.9272 against 0.90. The 38 foreign statements take part in both the calibration and the scoring of this reading, because a foreign cost on raw embeddings does not depend on the host document; readings without that overlap, at the registered document split: foreign set in disjoint halves 0.9559 and 0.9125, leave-one-foreign-statement-out 0.9052 and 0.9143 (verifier's recomputation); one statement moves a reading by 0.0116 to 0.0147. Clause (2) met by construction: 0 of 10 statements of the verbatim extract flagged under both cutoffs (highest cost 0.000691). Clause (3) met: balanced accuracy 0.9868 at the mean cutoff 0.34625 with no refit on the Wergeland source - 77 of 77 ibm statements flagged (lowest cost 0.3983), 37 of 38 own section statements not flagged; 36 of those 38 are copies of source statements, and with back-translated own statements the reading is the same 0.9868. Clause (3) exchanges the roles of the two articles of clause (1); no third article was read. Without a bar: the count rises by exactly k in 0.9107 of 392 insertion trials (0.9490, 0.9286, 0.8980, 0.8673 at k = 1 to 4); deleting supported statements leaves the count unchanged in 392 of 392 cases, by construction, while the mean of the same costs moves by 45.6% of one insertion; the 7 gold-tier documents read 0 to 3 flagged statements with nothing inserted (8 of 77 statements at the mean cutoff; adversarial tiers 9 of 17 and 0 of 4); against 1 - entailment on the 21 statements of the 4 adversarial summaries AUC 0.6545. The same flag after the shipped pre-pass reads 0.8168 and 0.7277 on clause (1), not met. With source statements under 5 words removed clauses (1) and (3) are unchanged and the extract reads 1 of 10 flagged (its markup statement ###), so one statement filter has to apply to document and source alike. Prediction: cutoff inside 0.33 to 0.45 - held; held-out balanced accuracy 0.90 to 0.96 - held; second article 0.85 to 0.95 - not held, above the interval; shipped balanced residual falls under the insertions - median per-document Spearman -0.7714 on the default call, -0.0143 with the pre-pass off. Guardrails: V 0/24, default-path and source-conditioned outputs bit-identical before and after, src, tests and docs trees unchanged; the mean nearest-source cost is a lower bound of the shipped balanced residual in 2508 of 2508 comparisons
- **Verdict** - Promoted; held-out balanced accuracy 0.9412 and 0.9272 against 0.90 (0.9052 and 0.9143 with each scored foreign statement left out of the calibration), verbatim extract 0 of 10 flagged, second article 0.9868 at the unchanged mean cutoff 0.34625 - raw embeddings, default reader, two articles

### E15-H79 matching-tie-break

- attachment: ../../notebooks/experiments/E15-H79-kj-matching-tie-break.ipynb sha256:5d34bca59ec5106a edited:2026-10-09T11:20:57Z
- attachment: ../../reports/E15-H79-matching-tie-break-metrics.json sha256:9dc9f546294baa2f edited:2026-10-09T11:20:54Z
- **Hypothesis** - because identical statements give the unit-capacity assignment equal costs and the solver then returns an arbitrary one of the optimal matchings (E13-H70: survivors recovered in 88/120 pure-deletion cases on a document with 9 repeated statements, matched order gap up to 0.3246 against 0.0587 shipped), adding a position term far below any cost difference between distinct statements (eps * |i/n - j/m|, eps = 1e-6) makes the assignment return the order-preserving matching among ties, so pure deletions on a document with repeated statements will never read above the shipped order gap, while every registered E13-H70 and E13-H71 reading stays unchanged at the single cutoff c = 0.325
- **Lever** - tie-break term in the assignment cost of the partial matching, eps = 1e-6 registered; the matching cutoff fixed at c = 0.325 (the E13-H71 value calibrated on half A and the in-sample optimum on all bases) for both the per-statement map and the matched order gap
- **Mechanism** - among statements with identical text every pairing costs the same, and for a convex position cost the cheapest assignment between two ordered groups is the monotone one, so the tie group is matched in document order; eps is six orders below the cutoff and cannot move a pairing between statements whose costs differ
- **Prediction** - with the tie-break the 120 pure-deletion cases on the 62-statement source document read at or below the shipped gap in 120/120 and the matched sub-sequences are textually equal in 120/120; the 280 registered deletion readings and the 1847 fully matched identities are unchanged; at c = 0.325 the count of fully matched equal-length pairs falls below 1847 by fewer than 20
- **Acceptance bar** - PASS iff, at c = 0.325 with eps = 1e-6: (1) on the E13-H70 third-pass set (62-statement source document with its 9 repeated statements, k = 2 interior deletions on prefixes, 120 cases) the matched order gap is at or below the shipped gap in 120/120 cases and the matched sub-sequence of the longer document is textually equal to the shorter document in >= 114/120; (2) >= 95% of the 280 order-preserving deletion cases read <= 0.005 and the deleted-only against reordered-and-deleted AUC is >= 0.95; (3) on every fully matched equal-length fixture pair the value is np.array_equal to shipped opw_gap; (4) the E13-H71 held-out macro-F1 at c = 0.325 stays >= 0.90 in both halves with the tie-break on
- **Experiment** - fixtures and case builders: reuse the code of notebooks/experiments/E13-H70-kj-matched-order-gap.ipynb (280 deletion cases, reorder ladder, third pass on the fixture document source) and notebooks/experiments/E13-H71-kj-partial-matching-map.ipynb (900 edited pairs, halves A and B) unchanged<br>arms: no tie-break (the E13 readings, reproduced first), eps = 1e-6; eps = 1e-4 and 1e-8 reported without a bar<br>reported without a bar: on pure deletions of the 53-statement document (repeated statements removed) the difference between the matched order gap and the identity gap opw_gap(B, B) of the shorter document; the 66 unequal-length natural pairs; the no-edit control of E13-H71 at c = 0.325<br>artefacts: notebooks/experiments/E15-H79-kj-matching-tie-break.ipynb, reports/E15-H79-matching-tie-break-metrics.json, logs/E15-H79-matching-tie-break.log<br>model: opus
- **Kill-gate** - harness check without the tie-break at c = 0.6: the E13-H70 third-pass readings must reproduce (survivors recovered 88/120, matched gap above shipped in 21/120); if they do not, the case builder differs from E13-H70 and the run is Blocked. Then the precondition: the cost between two repeated statements must be exactly equal across the tie group (bitwise) in at least one case - if no exact tie exists, a tie-break cannot be the fix and the hypothesis is killed
- **Evidence** - E13-H70 third pass (9 statements with the text '-', 32 of 43 deletions that removed a repeated statement not recovered); E13-H71 metrics (macro-F1 at fixed cutoffs: 0.325 reads 0.9674 / 0.9772 / 0.9723 on half A / half B / all, 0.6 reads 0.7771 on all); E13-H70 sensitivity (readings equal at c = 0.35, 0.45, 0.6; all 124 true pairs cost at most 0.322901)
- **Result** - Kill-gate passed: without the tie-break at c = 0.6 the E13-H70 third-pass readings reproduce (survivors recovered 88/120, matched gap above shipped 21/120) and the ground cost inside the group of repeated statements is bitwise equal in 120/120 cases. The copied builders reproduce 280/280, AUC 0.999903, 1847/1847, macro-F1 0.9674 and 0.9772. Clause (1) at c = 0.325, eps = 1e-06, 120 pure deletions on the 62-statement document with 9 repeated statements: matched order gap at or below the shipped gap in 120/120 cases (without the tie-break 99/120), above 0.005 in 0 cases (without it 23), largest reading 0.3246 → 0.004876; matched sub-sequence textually equal to the shorter document in 111/120 cases (without the tie-break 88/120), bar 114. The 9 misses read at most 0.003538: the tie group is matched in document order in 120/120 cases, but the position term also decides which repeated statement of the longer document stays unmatched and does not read which one was deleted (7 misses: another matching has a smaller position sum; 2: the sums are equal). eps = 1e-04, eps = 1e-08 and positions counted from 1 return the same counts. Clauses (2), (3), (4) met and not moved by the tie-break: 280/280 deletion cases <= 0.005 and AUC 0.999903, both np.array_equal to the E13-H70 readings; every fully matched equal-length pair equals shipped opw_gap, 1840/1840 at c = 0.325 - 7 of the 1847 pairs fully matched at c = 0.6 are no longer fully matched, so the sentence that every registered E13 reading stays unchanged at c = 0.325 does not hold for that count; macro-F1 0.9674 / 0.9772 with 0 of 21060 labels changed. The tie-break changes the matching in 35/120 cases of the document with repeated statements and in 0 of the 13223 cases of the other five sets. Outside the bar: on the 66 natural pairs of unequal length the cutoff 0.325 against 0.6 lowers the mean number of matched statements from 8.14 to 4.68 and the median matched gap from 0.0200 to 0.0000; with the shorter document encoded in its own call the clause (1) counts are the same. Guardrails: V 0/24, src and tests unchanged (docs not measured by the notebook)
- **Verdict** - Refuted; with the tie-break the matched sub-sequence is textually equal to the shorter document in 111/120 pure deletions with repeated statements, the bar needs 114 - the order-gap half of the clause is met (120/120 at or below shipped, largest reading 0.004876) and the single cutoff 0.325 keeps clauses (2) to (4)

### E15-H80 moved-on-matched-pairs

- attachment: ../../notebooks/experiments/E15-H80-kj-moved-on-matched-pairs.ipynb sha256:81717d84c23f301d edited:2026-10-09T11:26:47Z

- **Hypothesis** - because uniform marginals on unequal lengths force a statement without a counterpart to split its mass evenly over two targets (DEF-TRANS-13: rows of 1/24 + 1/24 on a 12 to 8 deletion), its aligned target, its displacement and its moved flag are set by float rounding, and its arbitrary target also shifts the ranks of the kept statements; reading moved on the matched pairs only - a statement holds a one-to-one match under the cutoff and its rank among the matched statements changed - will flag no statement on order-preserving deletions, equal the E12-H61 conjunction flag for flag on every fully matched equal-length pair, and keep at least 98% of the true relocations the conjunction flags when the relocated statements are reworded
- **Lever** - definition of the moved flag in the structural detail file: matched AND matched_displacement != 0 AND abs(matched_displacement / n_matched) > MOVED_TOLERANCE, where the matching is the unit-capacity partial matching at c = 0.325 with the tie-break eps = 1e-6 and matched_displacement is the rank of the matched target among matched targets minus the rank of the statement among matched statements; the E12-H61 conjunction (displacement != 0 AND abs(displacement_norm) > MOVED_TOLERANCE on the exact-plan alignment) is the baseline
- **Mechanism** - a one-to-one matching under a cutoff leaves a statement without a counterpart unmatched instead of assigning it a target, so no tie is read; ranks taken inside the matched sub-sequences do not move when unmatched statements are added or removed; on a fully matched equal-length pair the matching is the exact-plan permutation, so rank and norm are the shipped ones
- **Prediction** - on the 280 order-preserving deletion cases the conjunction flags at least 1 statement in at least 10% of the cases and the lever flags 0 statements in 280/280; flags equal on 1680/1680 verbatim permutations; on reworded reorders the lever keeps 99% to 100% of the true relocations the conjunction flags; the four E12-H61 fixtures read insertion 0/6, swap 2/2, reversal 12/12, shrinkage 0/12
- **Acceptance bar** - PASS iff, at c = 0.325 and eps = 1e-6: (1) on the 280 order-preserving deletion cases of E13-H70 the lever flags 0 statements, kept or removed, in 280/280 cases; (2) on every permutation of the verbatim reorder pool (1680, all fully matched) the lever's flags are np.array_equal to the conjunction's flags; (3) on reworded reorders - base document against the same 1680 permutations applied to its back-translation - the lever flags at least 98% of the truly relocated statements that the conjunction flags, pooled over the 10 bases; (4) the E12-H61 fixtures read insertion 0 of 6, swap 2 of 2, reversal 12 of 12 and the 12 to 8 text shrinkage 0 of 12
- **Experiment** - fixtures: data/processed/structure-fixture (10 bases of 11 to 14 statements, reorder_pool.json with 168 permutations per base, the <base>~bt back-translations, statement units pinned by encoding the stored statements); the 280 deletion cases built by the code of notebooks/experiments/E13-H70-kj-matched-order-gap.ipynb; the insertion, swap, reversal and shrinkage fixtures and the ground-truth relocation rule of notebooks/experiments/E12-H61-kj-moved-conjunction.ipynb, reused unchanged; encoder: shipped OpenVINO INT8<br>arms: shipped flag (norm only), E12-H61 conjunction (baseline), conjunction AND matched (reported without a bar), lever<br>reported without a bar: false fires per arm on kept and on removed statements of the 280 deletion cases; per-statement precision and recall of each arm against rank-among-survivors ground truth on reordered-and-deleted cases (the E13-H70 ladder, 2 deletions); false fires of each arm on statements that did not relocate in the reworded reorders; the lever with no tie-break; the share of deletion cases where two builds could differ (a removed statement whose exact-plan row holds two equal largest entries)<br>artefacts: notebooks/experiments/E15-H80-kj-moved-on-matched-pairs.ipynb, reports/E15-H80-moved-on-matched-pairs-metrics.json, logs/E15-H80-moved-on-matched-pairs.log<br>model: opus
- **Kill-gate** - the defect must reproduce beyond the E12-H61 synthetic: on the 280 order-preserving deletion cases the E12-H61 conjunction flags at least one statement (kept or removed) in at least one case; if it flags none, the lever has nothing to correct on real documents and the hypothesis is killed. Harness check first: the E12-H61 pool reading (13675 flagged true relocations, 0 lost under the conjunction) must reproduce, else Blocked
- **Evidence** - docs/defects-docdistance.md DEF-TRANS-13 (12 to 8 deletion: removed statements flagged moved 1 of 4 at commit bd0002e, 2 of 4 on re-execution with POT 0.9.6.post1; each removed row is two entries of 0.04167); E12-H61 (conjunction Promoted: 0 of 13675 true relocations lost, kept 0 of 8); E13-H70 (matched order gap equals the shipped gap on 1847/1847 fully matched pairs); E13-H71 (cutoff 0.325, all 124 true reworded pairs cost at most 0.322901)
- **Result** - Harness check passed: the E12-H61 pool reading reproduces (1680 permutations, 13675 truly relocated statements flagged, 0 lost under the conjunction) and the 280 deletion cases of E13-H70 rebuild to 280/280 at or below 0.005. Kill-gate passed: on the 280 order-preserving deletion cases the E12-H61 conjunction flags at least one statement in 246/280 cases (678 of 2772 kept and 472 of 700 removed statements; shipped norm-only flag 274/280 cases), and in 257 of the 280 cases its flag array changes with the choice among plan entries equal within 1e-12, so DEF-TRANS-13 reproduces on real documents. Lever as coded: matched AND matched_displacement != 0 AND abs(matched_displacement / n_matched) > MOVED_TOLERANCE AND NOT no_counterpart - the last conjunct is the shipped rule, kept in every arm, and it switched off 0 flags on the registered sets. Clause (1) met: 0 statements flagged in 280/280 deletion cases (by construction on this set: byte-identical survivors, no repeated statement). Clause (4) met: insertion 0 of 6, swap 2 of 2, reversal 12 of 12 on both reversal fixtures, text shrinkage 0 of 12 (conjunction 2 of 12). Outside the bar: reworded survivors after a deletion - lever 0/280 cases with a flag, conjunction 250/280; reordered-and-deleted ladder (11277 cases) - lever precision and recall 1.0000 against conjunction 0.7624 and 0.8779, with rank among survivors as ground truth, which is the lever's own definition; document with 9 repeated statements - lever flags a kept statement in 4/120 pure deletions with the tie-break and in 31/120 without it (conjunction 108/120); single transpositions at 14, 20 and 40 statements - flag arrays equal on 91/91, 183/190 and 765/780, the differences being statements at a rank step of exactly 5% of the length that the 1.1.5 float form j/n - i/n flags and shift/n does not; cutoff sensitivity of clause (3) - 0.9894 with 112 false fires at c = 0.32, 0.9769 at c = 0.30. - **Verdict** - Promoted; 0 statements flagged on 280/280 order-preserving deletions (conjunction: 246/280 cases with a flag), flags equal to the conjunction on 1680/1680 verbatim permutations, 13675/13675 reworded true relocations kept, fixtures 0/6, 2/2, 12/12, 0/12 - documents of 11 to 14 statements without repeated statements

### Results table (E15)

Executed 2026-10-09 as workflow runs `wf_9f856fe5-0c1` (H78, H79) and `wf_08927f54-c7e` (H80) - one opus executor and one methodologist verifier per hypothesis, no repair round needed. Tally: 2 Promoted, 1 Refuted; `V` 0/24 and default-path `smd` / `order_gap` bit-identical on every run; no file under `src/` changed.

| hypothesis | target KPI | key result | bar | verdict |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| E15-H78 raw nearest-source count | source residual (DEF-TRANS-8) | cutoffs 0.3375 / 0.3550 from the two calibration halves; held-out balanced accuracy 0.9412 / 0.9272 (0.9052 / 0.9143 with each scored foreign statement left out of the calibration); verbatim extract 0 of 10 flagged; Wergeland source 0.9868 at the mean cutoff 0.34625; count unchanged under deletions in 392/392 cases | held-out >= 0.90 in both directions, extract 0 flagged, second article >= 0.85 with the cutoff unchanged | Promoted (raw embeddings only: 0.8168 / 0.7277 after the pre-pass) |
| E15-H79 matching tie-break | partial matching with repeated statements | at c = 0.325, eps = 1e-06: matched gap at or below shipped in 120/120 pure deletions (99/120 without the tie-break), largest reading 0.3246 → 0.004876; matched sub-sequence textually equal in 111/120 (88/120 without); 280/280, AUC 0.999903, 1840/1840 identities, macro-F1 0.9674 / 0.9772 unchanged by the tie-break | 120/120 at or below shipped and >= 114/120 textually equal, E13 clauses held | Refuted (textual condition, 3 cases short) |
| E15-H80 moved on matched pairs | reporting fidelity (DEF-TRANS-13) | 0 statements flagged on 280/280 order-preserving deletions (conjunction: 246/280 cases, 678 kept and 472 removed statements); flags equal to the conjunction on 1680/1680 verbatim permutations; 13675/13675 reworded true relocations kept; fixtures 0/6, 2/2, 12/12, 0/12 | all four clauses | Promoted (documents of 11 to 14 statements without repeated statements) |

What the build takes from the batch. The unsupported-statement count is read on raw embeddings with the cutoff 0.34625 and never after the pre-pass. The partial matching uses the single cutoff 0.325 and carries the position term `1e-06 * |i/n - j/m|` as its rule among equal-cost matchings: the term is not a fix for repeated statements (E15-H79 Refuted), but without it the result among ties is the solver's choice (88/120 textually equal, 21 readings above the shipped gap). `moved` is read on the matched pairs. Three limits stay open and are documented, not solved: repeated statements (9/120 textual misses, a kept statement flagged in 4/120 pure deletions), a rank step of exactly 5% of the length (the matched form reads not moved where the 1.1.5 float form flags by rounding), and reworded statements that cost above the cutoff (the largest true reworded pair of the fixture lies 0.002099 below it).

## E16 - input-format-detection

One hypothesis, registered on 2026-10-10 after the user asked for autodetection of HTML, markdown and other input. At registration the build added the option `input_format` (`plain`, `markdown`, `html`, `auto`) with the default `plain`, and `auto` chose the reader from the content; E16-H81 asks whether that reader is a candidate default. After the run the user decided that the reader follows the file extension and that this rule is the default (log line of 2026-10-10 under the record); the content detector was removed. The block hash and the time of the registration are stored in [`E16-H81-registration.json`](../../reports/E13-campaign/results/E16-H81-registration.json), taken before the option was built.

### E16-H81 autodetected-reader-default

- attachment: ../../notebooks/experiments/E16-H81-kj-autodetected-reader-default.ipynb sha256:7a48cca9f4d486ad edited:2026-10-11T23:48:16Z

- **Hypothesis** - because the default prose reader returns markup markers as statements on a markdown document (ibm source: 21 of 70 statements hold no letter and no digit under the default reader, 0 of 48 under the markdown reader; review of 2026-10-10) and equal marker statements of two documents exchange mass at near-zero cost, a reader chosen per document from its content (input_format auto: markdown reader for markdown, HTML reader for HTML, prose reader otherwise) keeps the tier order of the ibm corpus with a band between the gold tier and the adversarial tier at least as wide as under the default reader, and reads an HTML render of a document as the same statements as its markdown original
- **Lever** - input_format auto of the build (detect_format, html_to_markdown, split_markdown) against the shipped default input_format plain, on the shipped OpenVINO INT8 path; this experiment changes no default and no threshold
- **Mechanism** - each statement carries 1/n of the transport mass; marker statements ('-', '###') are equal strings in every markdown document, so they move their share of the mass at near-zero cost and pull every distance between two markdown documents toward zero in proportion to their share; a structural reader drops them, so the distance is carried by content statements only. html_to_markdown writes the block structure of an HTML document as the lines the markdown reader reads, so the embedded texts of a render and of its original coincide
- **Prediction** - detection right on every fixture document; on the 10 non-reference ibm summaries against the gold reference the closeness under auto is lower than under plain for at least 9 of 10; the band [highest adversarial closeness, lowest gold-tier closeness] under auto is non-empty, at least as wide as the plain band of the same run (E12-H63 reads 0.7474 to 0.7944 on the shipped INT8 path) and lies 0.01 to 0.06 lower; Spearman of the 55 pair distances between the two readers at least 0.90; every HTML render reads the embedded statements of its markdown original
- **Acceptance bar** - PASS iff, on the shipped INT8 path: (1) detect_format returns markdown for the 12 ibm documents (11 summaries and the source), html for their 12 HTML renders and plain for the 12 texts made from the statements of the markdown reader joined by blank lines; (2) under auto every gold-tier summary reads a higher closeness to the gold reference than every adversarial summary, and the band between the two tiers is at least as wide as the band under plain in the same run; (3) the Spearman correlation of the 55 pair distances between plain and auto is at least 0.90; (4) for each of the 12 documents the embedded statement list of the HTML render under auto equals the list of the markdown original under auto, string for string, and the SMD between the two is at most 0.001; (5) guardrail: the 55 pair distances under plain equal the stored 1.1.5 baseline. Reported without a bar: the position of DEFAULT_THRESHOLD 0.77 against the auto band, the same two bands on the Wergeland article where its documents hold markup, n_unsupported and the match_status counts of the 7 gold-tier summaries under both readers, statement counts per document under both readers
- **Experiment** - fixtures: data/interim/exec-summaries/ibm-ai-adoption (11 summaries, source-article.md, tiers and reference as in notebooks/experiments/E12-H63-kj-semantic-threshold-band.ipynb), data/interim/E12-H67/allpairs-55.tsv, data/interim/ai-society-wergeland; HTML renders written by markdown_it (development environment only, not a package dependency) to data/interim/E16-H81/html/; plain controls written to data/interim/E16-H81/plain/. Arms: plain (shipped default), auto. Score through DocDistance on the OpenVINO INT8 backend; one encoder load, memo on. Tables: detection per document; statement counts and marker-statement counts per document and reader; closeness of the 10 summaries to the reference per reader with tier, band edges and the documents that set them; the 55 distances per reader with Spearman; the twin check per document; the readings without a bar<br>artefacts: notebooks/experiments/E16-H81-kj-autodetected-reader-default.ipynb, reports/E16-H81-autodetected-reader-default-metrics.json, logs/E16-H81-autodetected-reader-default.log<br>model: opus
- **Kill-gate** - before any distance: detect_format must return markdown for at least 11 of the 12 ibm documents (otherwise auto equals the default reader on this corpus and there is nothing to measure), and the default reader must return at least one marker statement on at least 1 of the 11 summaries (otherwise the mechanism is absent on the scored documents). Harness check first: the plain arm reproduces the 55 stored baseline distances and the band edges 0.7474 and 0.7944 of E12-H63 within 0.001
- **Evidence** - user request of 2026-10-10 (autodetection of HTML, markdown and other inputs); DEF-SEGM-12 and DEF-SEGM-15 in docs/defects-docdistance.md (markers kept as statements by the default reader; marker-only lines in the markdown reader); E12-H63 (DEFAULT_THRESHOLD 0.77 sits in the band 0.7474 to 0.7944 read with the default reader on INT8, set by adv2-b and haiku); review run wf_366ba93d-451 round 3 (21 of 70 and 0 of 48 on the ibm source). The per-pair outputs of the --markdown arm of reports/E13-campaign/checks/batch-memos/measure.py exist since stage B; only their sha256 digests were read before this registration
- **Result** - Harness check passed: the plain arm equals the 55 stored 1.1.5 pair distances (largest difference 0.0e+00) and reads the band 0.7474 (adv2-b) to 0.7944 (haiku). Kill-gate not passed: detect_format returns markdown for 12 of 12 ibm documents, and the default reader returns a marker statement on 0 of 11 summaries (0 of 98 statements; the gate requires at least 1 summary). The ibm source is the one ibm document with marker statements (21 of 70 under plain, 0 of 48 under auto) and it is in none of the 55 pairs. The five clauses were measured after the gate and do not decide the verdict: (1) detection right on 36 of 36 with the plain control built from the embedded statements, 35 of 36 with the reported statements (verifier's reading); (2) the auto band is 0.7474 to 0.7944, the same floats as under plain, because the reference and the two edge documents read the same statements under both readers; closeness under auto is lower for 2 of 10 summaries (opus -0.91 points, adv1-a -1.34), equal for 7 and higher for 1 (sonnet +0.01); 0 of 10 verdicts change at 0.77; (3) Spearman 0.9816 over the 55 pairs, 28 of 55 equal as floats, 0.9597 over the 27 that differ, largest difference 0.0322; (4) HTML render against markdown original under auto: embedded lists equal for 11 of 12 documents and SMD at most 0.001 for 11 of 12; the source differs in 1 of 48 statements (an emphasised line keeps its asterisks under the markdown reader, ground cost 0.1280) and reads SMD 2.856e-03; 11 of the 12 documents hold a title line and prose only; (5) the plain arm equals the baseline by np.array_equal. The readers differ on 3 of 11 summaries through segmentation, not through markers: the markdown reader gives each paragraph to the segmenter on its own. Outside the record: SMD(ibm source, Wergeland source) 0.6416 under plain and 0.5255 under auto. docdistance 1.1.21, V 0/24 under both readers, triangle violations 0/495 under both
- **Verdict** - Killed-at-gate; the default reader returns a marker statement on 0 of 11 scored summaries (the gate requires at least 1), so the registered mechanism is absent on the 55 pairs; detect_format returns markdown for 12 of 12 ibm documents. The run gives no evidence for a default of auto: the band is unchanged and 27 of 55 pair distances change by at most 0.0322 through paragraph-wise segmentation
