### Results (E16)

| hypothesis | lever | key numbers | bar | verdict |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| E16-H81 autodetected reader as default | reader (`input_format` auto) | kill-gate: marker statement on 0 of 11 scored summaries (source 21 of 70, in none of the 55 pairs); side readings: detection 36 of 36, band 0.7474 to 0.7944 under both readers, 0 of 10 verdicts change at 0.77, Spearman 0.9816 (27 of 55 pairs differ, largest 0.0322), HTML twins equal for 11 of 12 (source SMD 2.856e-03) | tier order kept and band at least as wide, Spearman >= 0.90, twins equal with SMD <= 0.001 | Killed-at-gate |

Executed 2026-10-10 as workflow run `wf_86296670-5d0` - one opus executor and one methodologist verifier, no repair round. Tally: 1 Killed-at-gate; `V` 0/24 under both readers; the plain arm equals the 55 stored 1.1.5 distances.

## Lessons learned

- **Baseline near the ceiling for ordering** - perfect ordinality and `d' = 4.60` leave little room; resolution (dynamic range), not the normalized boundary, is the axis with room
- **Anisotropy is the bottleneck** - the one lever that helps removes a single common principal component, de-bunches the cosines, triples dynamic range
- **Number-aware weighting is self-defeating on number-heavy sources** - both the faithful and the info-noise tiers carry the article's percentages, so up-weighting numbers pulls adversarial summaries toward gold
- **A wider mean gap is not a wider boundary** - E01-H1 raises `R` while the boundary margin turns negative; `V` catches what `R` hides
- **Heavier machinery did not pay** - unbalanced OT (non-metric, ~120x) and tail aggregation (noise at ~12 statements) both underperform the cheap exact mean
- **Conditioning on the source separates failure modes (E02)** - the symmetric distance cannot tell info-loss from fabrication; re-basing the transport onto `S` and adding a grounding axis does, the source-conditioned design's central claim confirmed
- **Single-premise NLI mis-grades compression (E02)** - a faithful summary statement fuses several source sentences, so no single source premise entails it; top-k joint-premise aggregation is required, not optional (R1 muddied, R2 fixed it)
- **General NLI is weak on numbers (E02)** - the contradiction signal barely fires even on fabricated forecasts, so the grounding residual rides on the ungrounded component - a numeric-aware verifier is the open gap
- **Relevance, not entailment, separates compression from fabrication (E03)** - gating the ungrounded mass by max reranker relevance closes the gold intrusion the entailment residual could not, because faithful compression has high relevance and low entailment while fabrication has both low
- **Number density defeats the numeric verifier (E03)** - on an 82-figure source, whole-source matching lets fabricated numbers match by coincidence (Set 2 collapses to 0.05) while top-k localized matching flags faithful restatements whose aligned source is elsewhere (gold rises to 0.08); the same density that makes numbers the adversarial signal makes them un-verifiable here
- **The cross-encoder reranker is load-bearing (E03)** - the bi-encoder cosine neither shortlists faithfully (recall@10 of top-3 is 0.58) nor replaces relevance (Spearman 0.40); the 60% reranker cost is structural to the grounding design, not removable with the embeddings already on hand
- **Separability is not correct ordering (E03)** - the symmetric SMD separates Set 1 from Set 2 on this fixture but inverts the severity (info-loss read as more divergent than fabrication); the conditioned blend's win is the correct ordering and the axis attribution, which a single source-blind scalar cannot give regardless of separability
- **Anisotropy carries to the conditioned axis (E04)** - the E01-H3 lever, applied to the conditioned `D_sel` over the pooled {A,B,S} corpus, widens dynamic range 3.39x at `0` violations, a larger gain than on the symmetric distance (2.7x) because the conditioned coverage profiles start more bunched; like E01-H3 it is resolution not a sharper boundary (contrast unchanged)
- **Coverage temperature trades contrast for range (E04)** - sharpening the soft source assignment spreads the distances but within-tier, so a lower `τ` raises DR while lowering the gold/adversarial contrast and breaking ordinality; the predicted contrast gain is the wrong direction
- **The cross-encoder is load-bearing against its own family (E04)** - a distilled same-family `bge-reranker-base` recalls only 0.55 of the `v2-m3` top-3 and changes the ranking (Spearman 0.70); even keeping cross-attention, a smaller reranker is not a faithful replacement
- **A cross-encoder cascade is the closest faithful speed-up (E04)** - a tiny MiniLM pre-filter clears the fidelity (Spearman 0.98) and speed (2.3x) bars where the E03 bi-encoder could not (0.55), but at `m = 15` still drops 13% of the top-3 evidence and adds a gold intrusion; the reranker cost stands, the cascade is the open direction
- **The cross-encoder floor holds across six candidate classes (E05)** - two multilingual cross-encoders (mmarco-MiniLM 118M recall@3 0.405, jina 278M 0.599), late-interaction MaxSim (0.47) and a learned-sparse proxy (0.08) all fail to recall the `v2-m3` top-3 (bar 0.90), jina the closest at `D_grd` Spearman 0.927; with E03 (bi-encoder) and E04 (distilled, English MiniLM) that is six scorer classes - the cross-encoder is irreplaceable on this fixture
- **Padding, not precision, is the free CPU lever (E05)** - the reranker pairs carry 2.33x padded-token waste; length-bucketing cuts the CPU INT8 reranker 42.5% at score Spearman 0.99996 over the full 6,860-pair grid (E06-H25), a real free win short of the 50% bar E05 scored it against; the grid is compute-bound (an encoder, no KV cache), so lower precision is not the lever
- **A redundant source is not a compressible one (E05)** - 91% of the 70 source statements have a `>= 0.85` neighbour (`k_eff` 10), but the redundancy is soft: clustering to half (k=35) drops top-3 recall to 0.76 and breaks the grounding ranking - near-duplicate sources are interchangeable for retrieval but not collapsible without losing the fine distinctions `D_grd` needs; index-recall understates fidelity here, so `D_grd` Spearman is the truer measure
- **Training does not lift late interaction past the gate (E06)** - a trained multilingual ColBERT (jina-colbert-v2) MaxSim recall@3 read `0.4456` on the first run and `0.5408` on the re-run with later model code, against the untrained mmBERT proxy `0.47` and the `0.60` kill-gate; retrieval fine-tuning adds at most 0.07 of recall on this source-conditioned grounding task, so the E05 "untrained backbone" caveat is closed
- **Learned-sparse buys recall without the order (E06)** - trained bge-m3 sparse recall@15 `0.867` beats the cosine floor `0.643` and the shortlist reaches 0.95 at `m = 33`, but its `D_grd` Spearman is `+0.770` against the `0.95` bar; lexical term-weight scoring catches the overlap the pooled vector dropped yet does not reproduce the document order, so no cascade - the recall-vs-order split of E03, again
- **The cross-encoder floor holds against trained scorers too (E06)** - with E03 (bi-encoder), E04 (distilled, English MiniLM) and E05 (multilingual cross-encoders, untrained late-interaction / sparse) already ruled out, E06 adds trained multilingual late-interaction and trained learned-sparse; eight scorer classes, none matches the v2-m3 ranking - the grounding cost is structural, not a tuning gap
- **A flash-attention scorer NaNs on CPU padding (E06)** - jina-colbert-v2's flash-attn XLM-R CPU forward returns NaN over padded positions, so a default padded batch silently corrupts the MaxSim grid (an all-zero grid masked by `nan_to_num`, a near-random recall@3 `0.003`); encoding one sequence at a time (`batch_size=1`) removes the padding and fixes it - a methodological caveat for any CPU late-interaction encode
- **On unequal lengths the order gap reads the surplus, not the order (E13)** - uniform marginals force the statements without a counterpart off the diagonal, so 280 of 280 order-preserving deletions read above the paraphrase bar; the same `opw_gap` taken over one-to-one matched pairs reads all 280 at or below 0.005 and still separates a reorder after a deletion at AUC 0.999903 (E13-H70)
- **One-to-one matching under a cutoff labels kept, removed and added (E13)** - held-out macro-F1 0.9772 / 0.9457 against 0.3130 / 0.3266 for the shipped flags, which on these edits equal a classifier that calls every statement kept; one cutoff, 0.325, serves the map and the matched order gap (E13-H71, E15-H79)
- **A mean moves under deletions, a count does not (E13, E15)** - the mean cost to the nearest source statement reads a verbatim extract correctly (0.9999) but moves by 34.1% to 45.6% of one insertion when supported statements are deleted; the count of statements above a cutoff is unchanged in 392 of 392 deletion cases and flags foreign statements at held-out balanced accuracy 0.9412 / 0.9272 (E13-H73, E15-H78)
- **The pre-pass removes the signal the source-support reading needs (E13, E15)** - the nearest-source cost separates own from foreign statements at AUC 0.9670 on raw embeddings and 0.7889 after the anisotropy pre-pass, and the count fails its bar after the pre-pass (0.8168 / 0.7277); one result therefore reads two geometries, the pre-pass one for `D_sel` and the raw one for the count
- **The order gap has no plateau (E13)** - it grows +190.5% from displacement 60 to 200 and is linear in the count of displaced statements (R^2 0.999); the kernel reach of about 54 positions does not cap it, and `opw_gap(P, P)` is not zero at length (0.0068 at 53 statements, up to 0.010 at 300) (E13-H74, E13-H70)
- **A cap on the ground cost has too few arcs to act on (E13)** - only 30.0% of cross-article insertions cost above 0.6 to their nearest host statement (5.2% on statements of 5+ words), so the cap was killed at its gate (E13-H72)
- **The attribution of the pre-pass gain depends on the path (E14)** - mean subtraction carries the gain on the symmetric panels of both articles (103.7% and 102.8%), while on the conditioned path of the ibm fixture centering alone reads 0.5471x the shipped range; the projection step there removes the direction of the one-word markup statements, not content of the source (E14-H75, E14-H76)
- **A kernel adds nothing at pre-determined bandwidths (E14)** - a wide RBF kernel is close to affine in the cosine and reproduces the linear pre-pass (contrast gain 3.1% at m = 0.25 against the required 10%), a narrow one puts most statement pairs at nearly the same distance and the margins fall to zero or below (E14-H77)
- **Markup statements are one cause behind four readings (E13 to E15)** - the plain reader, the default up to build 1.1.24, turns 21 of the 70 ibm source statements into bare list and heading markers; they repeat, so one-to-one matching is not unique (88 of 120 deletions recovered), they carry 0.68 of the centered pool variance, and they set the capped-cost and foreign-statement readings; markdown-aware segmentation leaves none (DEF-SEGM-12)
- **A position term orders ties but does not name the deleted member (E15)** - with `1e-06 * |i/n - j/m|` in the assignment cost the tie group matched in document order on the 120 measured cases, which is not a general rule (tie condition: ACC-TRANS-83), and every pure deletion reads at or below the shipped gap (120/120, largest 0.004876), but the matched sub-sequence is textually equal in 111 of 120 cases, three short of the bar; which repeated statement stays unmatched needs the neighbouring statements, a mechanism not measured here (E15-H79)
- **A flag read from the exact plan on unequal lengths is set by float ties (E15)** - a statement without a counterpart splits its mass into equal parts, so the argmax target and the `moved` flag change with the solver build (257 of 280 deletion cases hold such a choice); read on matched pairs the flag fires in 0 of 280 cases and keeps every true relocation of the fixture (E15-H80, DEF-TRANS-13)
- **Several registered clauses could not fail (E13 to E15)** - a verbatim extract against its own source, byte-identical survivors of a deletion and verbatim permutations hold by construction once the code is correct; the verifiers flagged each, and the readings that could fail (reworded survivors, repeated statements, a second article, leave-one-out) were added beside the bars - the next registration puts the bar on those sets
- **A mechanism is read on the scored documents before it is registered (E16)** - the marker statements that motivated E16-H81 were read on the ibm source (21 of 70); the 11 scored summaries hold none, so the kill-gate fired. The two readers still differ on 3 of 11 summaries, through segmentation: the markdown reader gives each paragraph to the segmenter on its own

## Conclusions

- **Ships** - baseline exact SMD: metric, 0.08 ms/pair, perfect ordinality
- **Optional** - anisotropy removal (k=1) as a resolution pre-pass, ~2x latency, `d'` caveat noted
- **Thin margin is intrinsic** - all eleven summaries describe one article and share its content, so the boundary is genuinely narrow
- **Source-conditioned axes (E02)** - `D_sel` ships as the selection axis (clean, metric, sub-ms, 0 violations); `D_grd` (R2 joint premise) is a tier-level fabrication flag, ~109 s/pair - E03 turned it into a per-document metric with the H11 relevance-gate, not the numeric verifier E02 expected
- **Grounding axis fix (E03)** - the H11 relevance-gated ungrounded mass closes the per-document gold intrusion (2 → 0) at no extra cost, turning `D_grd` from a tier flag into a per-document discriminator on this fixture; ship it as the grounding-axis definition
- **Blended scalar (E03)** - the H14 blend `α·D_sel + (1−α)·D_grd` (`α ∈ [0.6, 0.9]`) orders the two failure modes correctly (Set 2 fabrication above Set 1 info-loss) where the symmetric scalar inverts them - a single interpretable conditioned scalar; the win is severity ordering and attribution, and needs a second source before it is trusted
- **Not shipped (E03)** - the numeric verifier (defeated by source figure density) and the two reranker-cost levers (cosine neither shortlists nor replaces the cross-encoder); the ~109 s/pair grounding cost stands
- **Resolution lever ships as the default (E04)** - anisotropy removal on the conditioned `D_sel` (`k = 1`, now the shipped default, opt-out via `anisotropy=False`) widens dynamic range 3.39x at `0` violations, with the E01-H3 caveat that it spreads the band rather than sharpening the boundary
- **Reranker cost still stands (E04)** - neither a distilled replacement (recall 0.55, Spearman 0.70) nor a cross-encoder cascade (recall 0.87 at `m = 15`, one new intrusion) clears the correctness guardrails; the ~109 s/pair grounding cost is unmoved, the cascade the most promising future lever
- **CPU speed: the floor holds, one free win (E05)** - no lever clears the `>= 50%` CPU cut at a preserved verdict; the v2-m3 cross-encoder is reconfirmed irreplaceable (no smaller cross-encoder, late-interaction or sparse scorer recalls its top-3), jina-reranker-v2 the closest (Spearman 0.9636 but recall@3 0.599, short of the 0.90 bar; see the E05-H20 note). The one shippable improvement is length-bucketing - a `42.5%` CPU cut at score Spearman 0.99996, `7` points short of the bar; ship it as a portable default and keep v2-m3 as the reranker
- **Trained scorers do not move the grounding floor (E06)** - a trained multilingual ColBERT (recall@3 `0.4456`, `0.5408` on the re-run, kill-gate `0.60`) and a trained learned-sparse bge-m3 (recall@15 `0.867` but Spearman `+0.770` against `0.95`) both fail to replace v2-m3 or cascade; the open E05 direction is closed, the cross-encoder grounding cost is structural across eight scorer classes
- **One CPU win ships (E06)** - length-bucketing, re-scored end-to-end over all 11 documents under the looser lift gate, cuts CPU INT8 reranker latency `42.5%` (`42.1%` on the re-run of 2026-10-09) at score Spearman `0.99996` / `D_grd` Spearman `1.0` and `0` intrusion change; promoted from the E05 refuted-near-miss to the reserved CPU-speed candidate and wired into the shipped statement encoder (`OpenVINOEncoder` / `TorchEncoder`), where the same padded-batch waste lives
- **Calibration is documented, not re-tuned (E12)** - the thresholds 0.77 and 0.90 keep their values. The readings behind that: no structural threshold is safe on both articles (0 of 29, band empty by 0.0019, E12-H59); no matched statement costs above the 0.6 no-counterpart line (corpus maximum 0.5774, E12-H60); the 0.77 band edges move +2.30 / -1.64 points under leave-one-out and INT8 drift is 40.6% of a margin (E12-H63); the pre-pass needs a pool of at least 8 ibm documents, about 71 statements (E12-H65)
- **Two runtime memos (E12)** - the per-document embed memo reads 9.94x on the 55-row sweep (10.12x on the re-run of 2026-10-09) and the per-(document, source) grounding-grid memo 10.03x (9.66x on the re-run), both at byte-identical output; a batch without repeated documents gains nothing (control 1.00x) (E12-H67, E12-H68)
- **The order gap gets a matched reading (E13)** - `matched_order_gap`, the shipped `opw_gap` over one-to-one matched pairs under the cutoff 0.325, reads 280 of 280 order-preserving deletions at or below 0.005 where the shipped gap reads 0 of 280; `order_gap`, `structure_closeness` and the structural verdict keep their values (E13-H70)
- **Detail files label kept, removed and added (E13)** - the same matching labels statements at held-out macro-F1 0.9772 / 0.9457 against 0.3130 / 0.3266 for the shipped flags (E13-H71)
- **`moved` is read on matched pairs (E15)** - the one shipped field whose definition changes: 0 flags on 280 of 280 order-preserving deletions against 246 cases with flags under the 1.1.5 conjunction, and every true relocation of the fixture kept (E15-H80, DEF-TRANS-13)
- **The source residual gets a count beside it (E15)** - the count of statements whose nearest source statement costs more than 0.34625 on raw embeddings: held-out balanced accuracy 0.9412 / 0.9272, 0.9868 on a second article, 0 of 10 flagged on a verbatim extract; `residual_a` and `residual_b` keep their values and are no longer described as the unsupported share of a document (E15-H78, DEF-TRANS-8)
- **The pre-pass stays as shipped (E14)** - centering carries the gain on the symmetric panels of two articles (103.7%, 102.8%), but centering alone reads 0.5471x the shipped range on the conditioned path, so the projection step stays; a kernel pre-pass adds nothing at five pre-determined bandwidths (E14-H75, E14-H76, E14-H77)
- **Not built (E13)** - a capped ground cost (killed at its gate: 30.0% of cross-article insertions cost above the cap), the mean nearest-source residual (moves by 34.1% of one insertion under deletions) and an order-kernel plateau (the gap grows +190.5% from displacement 60 to 200)
- **Input format (E16)** - the build reads HTML through a conversion to markdown lines and chooses the reader per document. In the experiment `auto` chose it from the content; by the user's decision of 2026-10-10 it is chosen from the file extension and `auto` is the default, with `input_format="plain"` for the 1.1.5 reading. On the ibm corpus `auto` leaves the tier band unchanged (0.7474 to 0.7944) and changes 27 of 55 pair distances by at most 0.0322; an HTML render reads the statements of its markdown original for 11 of 12 documents, and the twelfth differs in one emphasised line. E16-H81 was killed at its gate, so these are side readings
- **Limits that stay open (E13 to E15)** - repeated statements (matched sub-sequence textually equal in 111 of 120 deletions), a rank step of exactly 5% of the length, reworded statements that cost above the cutoff (the largest true reworded pair of the fixture lies 0.002099 below it), and both cutoffs measured on one article with one reader setting; the marker statements of the plain reader get a warning, not a fix (DEF-SEGM-12)

## Next steps

- **Promote the E03 winners** - wire the H11 relevance-gated ungrounded mass into the grounding axis as `D_grd`, and expose the H14 blend (`α ≈ 0.75`) as the single source-conditioned scalar; the per-document intrusion is closed and the failure modes order correctly
- **E04 resolution lever shipped** - the H15 anisotropy pre-pass (`k = 1`) is now the default on the conditioned `D_sel` (`anisotropy=False` to opt out), wired through the library (`compute_source_conditioned`, `distance_wrt_source`) and the CLI (`--no-anisotropy`); folded into the SOTA design doc alongside the symmetric E01-H3 anisotropy
- **E04 cascade near-miss, closed by E05** - the E04-H18 MiniLM→`v2-m3` cascade was the open direction (recall@15 0.87); E05 tested a larger `m` and a multilingual pre-filter (mmarco-MiniLM) and they fail too - jina-reranker-v2 only reaches recall@m 0.95 at `m = 34` (half the source), so no cascade clears the gate with a real saving
- **Cross-fixture check (parked, separate fixture)** - the H14 win is severity ordering and attribution on one article, where the symmetric scalar also clears 0/28 gold-vs-adv; a second-source fixture is prepared separately later, then E02/E03/E04 re-run on it before claiming the blend beats symmetric in general; E04 itself is scored on the existing fixture meanwhile
- **Anisotropy continuation (parked, E01 lineage)** - still open: sweep the anisotropy `k` further and test a gentle numeric weight tuned to preserve `V` stacked on anisotropy removal; source-free, distinct from E04-H15, which applies anisotropy removal to the conditioned `D_sel`, not the symmetric distance
- **E06 closes the trained-scorer thread** - no trained multilingual late-interaction or learned-sparse model clears the cross-encoder floor; the only open grounding-speed lever is the E04-H18 cross-encoder cascade on a faithful pre-filter, not a cheaper scorer
- **Length-bucketing wired into the statement encoder (E06-H25)** - the lever now batches `OpenVINOEncoder` / `TorchEncoder` by token length; the `42.5%` is reranker-grid-measured, the statement-encoder CPU cut is benchmarked separately in [`notebooks/10-kj-encoder-length-bucketing.ipynb`](../../notebooks/10-kj-encoder-length-bucketing.ipynb)
- **Open grounding cost** - the reranker grid stands as the ~99% bottleneck (~67 s/pair shipped CPU; the ~109 s figure includes the diagnostic R1 NLI sweep the shipped `D_grd` skips); E03/E04/E05 ruled out six scorer classes (bi-encoder, distilled and multilingual cross-encoders, late-interaction, learned-sparse) - none recalls the v2-m3 top-3; the structural wins are length-bucketing (42.5%, shippable) and source clustering (36% safe ceiling), neither alone reaching 50%
- **Ship the E05 length-bucketing win** - length-sort the reranker pair list and tighten `max_length` to the per-call 100th-percentile pair length; a `42.5%` CPU cut at score Spearman 0.99996, portable, the one E05 lever worth wiring into the grounding chain when it lands in the library
- **E05 open replacement directions** - jina-reranker-v2 (278M) is the closest faithful cross-encoder (Spearman 0.9636 but recall@3 0.5986, short of the 0.90 bar); a mid-size multilingual cross-encoder is untested and is the open scorer hunt; the *trained* multilingual ColBERT and learned-sparse scorers were tested in E06 and do not clear the floor (E06-H26, E06-H27)
- **Refuted, do not revisit** - salience / numeric weighting (breaks ordinality on number-heavy sources), angular cost (null), unbalanced residual (worse, slow, non-metric), tail aggregation (noise at this statement count), single-premise grounding (mis-grades compression, superseded by R2 joint premise), numeric verifier (defeated by source figure density, E03-H10), bi-encoder cascade / replacement (cosine neither shortlists nor replaces the cross-encoder, E03-H12/H13), smaller / distilled / multilingual cross-encoder replacement (E04-H17, E05-H20 - none recalls the v2-m3 top-3), late-interaction MaxSim and learned-sparse over an untrained backbone (E05-H21/H22 - below the gate) and their trained versions (E06-H26 killed at its gate, E06-H27 refuted), source-statement clustering past ~36% (E05-H24 - breaks the grounding ranking)
- **Cutoff transfer (E13, E15)** - the matching cutoff 0.325 was measured on the ibm fixture alone and the source-support cutoff 0.34625 on ibm with one check on the Wergeland source; the next fixture is a third article with reworded and repeated statements, and its registration puts the bars on the sets that can fail (reworded survivors, repeated statements, leave-one-out). The review of 2026-10-10 adds four requirements for that registration, each from a scratch reading that no notebook computes: the bar of a held-out balanced accuracy sits on an interval bound, because the statement-level bootstrap intervals of E15-H78 (0.8824 to 0.9853 and 0.8675 to 0.9767, 20000 resamples) include its bar 0.90; an insertion clause with reworded kept statements and donors from the same article, where `moved` flagged a statement in 25 of 150 cases (1.1.5 rule: 123 of 150) because an inserted statement took the match of a kept one; a reworded class written by a language model, because the reworded class of E13-H71 is per-statement back-translation only (on the 4 regenerated pairs of the structure fixture 5 of 35 statements read `removed` and 9 of 39 `added`); the minimum pool 71 of the anisotropy pre-pass on a second article and on the INT8 encoder (bootstrap stability 0.6313 on the 76-statement Wergeland pool against 0.9791 on the 98-statement ibm pool, E14-H77 metrics)
- **Repeated statements (E15)** - which of several equal statements stays unmatched cannot be read from the cost and the position alone; a term that reads the neighbouring statements is the untested mechanism (E15-H79). Two readings from the build of 2026-10-10, taken with scratch scripts and not registered, name two further mechanisms to test: the position term `abs(i/n - j/m)` is convex but not strictly convex, so the in-order and the crossed pairing of tied copies can cost the same (tail truncation with an adjacent repeated statement: 2690 of 5555 synthetic cases crossed, 0 of 5555 with a squared term); and the float32 ground cost of two identical rows reads 0 or 3.45e-04, a difference above the position term of 1e-06. A third reading of the review of 2026-10-10, also not registered: on a document compared with a bit-equal copy of itself the matching returned crossed copies in 3 of 1000 synthetic documents and 2 of 120 encoded documents, and in 0 of 1000 with a float64 ground cost; a float64 cost is a candidate lever, with a bar on the E15-H79 fixture (111 of 120 against 114) and on identity pairs
- **Reading of `.md` inputs (E13 to E15)** - the plain reader, the default up to build 1.1.24, turns 21 of the 70 ibm source statements into marker statements, and they set four readings; whether `.md` inputs are read markdown-aware by default is a product decision, the library only warns (DEF-SEGM-12); on 2026-10-10 the user asked for autodetection of the input format: the build adds `input_format` (`plain`, `markdown`, `html`, `auto`) with the default `plain`, and E16-H81 measures the reader chosen by content as a candidate default. E16-H81 was killed at its gate (the scored summaries hold no marker statement). The user then decided that the reader follows the file extension by default. Open for a next registration: documents that both hold markers (the one cross-article pair read 0.6416 under plain and 0.5255 under auto, outside the record), inline markup that the markdown reader keeps as characters (the twin that failed), HTML from a second generator, and fixtures whose plain controls are fixed in the record
- **Closed in E12 to E15, do not revisit without a new mechanism** - refuted: norm-mass transport marginals (E12-H66, null), the mean nearest-source residual (E13-H73), an order-kernel plateau (E13-H74), a kernel PCA pre-pass at pre-determined bandwidths (E14-H77); killed at a gate: a relevance gate of another form (E12-H69), a capped ground cost (E13-H72); rejected on a confirmed reading: a centering-only pre-pass on the conditioned path (E14-H76)
- **Structural calibration on statements as the command reads them (DEF-DOCS-18)** - `STRUCTURE_THRESHOLD` 0.90 was placed on the stored statements of `data/processed/structure-fixture/statements.json`, which keep the title line of each ibm summary as statement 0 (E12-H59: lowest un-reordered ibm pair 0.906144, 0 of 72 flagged); the package removes the title line from a file with every reader, and `distance-structural` on the 55 pairs of ibm summary files (`data/interim/E12-H67/allpairs-55.tsv`) reads `structure_closeness` below 0.90 on 3 pairs with `input_format="plain"` and on 4 with the default reader, lowest 0.897057 (builds 1.1.29 to 1.1.31, which share one package source; the count and the lowest value equal the stored 1.1.5 outputs in `reports/E13-campaign/baseline/default-path.json.gz`, and two of the three values differ from the stored ones in the fourth decimal, because `structure_closeness` depends on the argument order and the stored file keeps each pair in sorted name order). A second difference is the encoder: E12-H59 encoded the stored statements with the torch backend, the command encodes with OpenVINO INT8, and on the stored statements with the title line removed the backend alone reads gold against gold-2 at 0.900150 (torch) against 0.897407 (OpenVINO, the command's value in the order of the pair list). A calibration on statements as the command reads them and with the encoder the command uses is a hypothesis of its own: the ladder is rebuilt on the read statements with OpenVINO INT8, and the value is tested two-sided as in E12-H59 (held-out reordered rungs read not similar, held-out un-reordered pairs read similar) on summaries or paraphrases that are in none of the 55 pairs that set it. Whether it is registered is the user's decision; 0.90 is not moved until then
- **Registration record** - from E16-H81 on, the sha256 of a registered block and its UTC time are stored outside the ledger before the lever is built or run (`reports/E13-campaign/results/E16-H81-registration.json`); the registrations of E13-H70 to E15-H80 sit in the same uncommitted change as their results and carry dates without times (review of 2026-10-10)
